<a class=\"vocab\" href=\"#word-3847\" data-words=\"3847\">He</a> <a class=\"vocab\" href=\"#word-7596\" data-words=\"7596\">sat</a> <a class=\"vocab\" href=\"#word-928\" data-words=\"928\">beside</a> <a class=\"vocab\" href=\"#word-3891\" data-words=\"3891\">her</a>. &quot;<a class=\"vocab\" href=\"#word-4062\" data-words=\"4062\">I</a> <a class=\"vocab\" href=\"#word-7458\" data-words=\"7458\">shall</a> <a class=\"vocab\" href=\"#word-5452\" data-words=\"5452\">need</a> <a class=\"vocab\" href=\"#word-3884\" data-words=\"3884\">help</a> <a class=\"vocab\" href=\"#word-9260\" data-words=\"9260\">with</a> <a class=\"vocab\" href=\"#word-8385\" data-words=\"8385\">the</a> <a class=\"vocab\" href=\"#word-3378\" data-words=\"3378\">forms</a>.&quot;</p><section class=\"exercise\"><h2>Reading Practice</h2><p class=\"meta\">先完成选择，再查看解析。练习内容不计入词汇正文覆盖。</p><p class=\"meta\" data-score=\"29\"></p><button type=\"button\" data-reset=\"29\">清空本章作答，重新自测</button><div class=\"question\" id=\"q-30-1\" data-question=\"29,0\"><p class=\"meta\">1. Qualification / evidence</p><p><strong>Why does Mara restrain her excitement when the screen shows nineteen housing cases?</strong></p><div class=\"options\"><button type=\"button\" data-option=\"0\">A. The corrected rent calculation has made the medical records irrelevant.</button><button type=\"button\" data-option=\"1\">B. The housing officer has prohibited any further investigation into the cases.</button><button type=\"button\" data-option=\"2\">C. A shared number does not establish that the housing claims involve the nineteen referred patients.</button><button type=\"button\" data-option=\"3\">D. She already knows that the housing and medical records refer to entirely separate people.</button></div><div class=\"feedback\" hidden=\"hidden\"></div></div></section><nav class=\"chapter-nav\" aria-label=\"Chapter navigation\"><button data-go=\"28\">← Previous</button><button data-go=\"30\">Next →</button></nav></article>"},{"id":"v03c07","title":"7. The Performance That Changed Its Plan","volume":"3","anchor":"chapter-0031","paragraphs":["The theatre's first reopening notice contained a mistake. It promised a benefit performance by \"the grateful patients of Mercy House.\" Mrs Bell crossed out three words. The performers were neighbors who had volunteered; some were patients, some were staff, and one was a plumber with a powerful singing voice. Gratitude was neither an entry requirement nor the plot. June photographed the correction and then put her camera away as she had promised.","It was early November, and the northeast wind worried the windows. Rain beat against the glass in a torrent, turning the courtyard path into a shallow stream. Ivo checked that the drain would not overflow, while Mara laid a dry blanket over a spare chair. The weather would affect the audience. It would not excuse blocking an emergency exit with donated furniture. She moved the couch herself, then called for help when it would not fit through the door.","\"A triumph of cooperation over doorways,\" Cassian observed. He took the other end after she nodded. They had become proficient at small questions, though neither could yet claim perfection. A volunteer asked whether he would preside over the evening. \"Mrs Bell has that honor,\" he said. The retiree looked up from the program. \"Honor? I was promised tea.\"","The stage manager had marked a metre-wide route behind the curtain. Nothing could be placed there, even temporarily. A civilian fire officer inspected the route and checked the gas cutoff. The inspection had no glamour, but a clear exit could play a major role when a crowd needed to leave. A show would not become safer because a courageous woman had decided she could carry everyone out herself. Mara accepted the warning without pretending she enjoyed it.","A child named Pip had drawn a panorama of the hospital. The people were taller than the building. An enormous bronze springbok stood on its roof. Mrs Bell had sold the real one to a local collector, who allowed Pip to borrow it for the drawing. \"An exceptional animal,\" Cassian said. Pip said it could jump over a jet. \"I fear I have underestimated it.\" The boy offered to teach him the difference between ordinary and heroic wildlife.","Behind the scenery, Mara found the former contractor's advertising banner. Its declaration of \"normal memory for every age\" had been painted over a garden scene. Its ancestry claim had not disappeared with the words; the original outline remained visible under ultraviolet light. A promise could outdate the evidence behind it while leaving its image in place. Mara wanted the banner preserved for the file, so Ivo rolled it around a clean tube rather than tearing it down.","Noor asked her to stop using the entire theatre as an evidence repository. The poster was dry and could be stored in a separate room; a clinical document required different protection. Their need to uncover wrongdoing did not suspend the need to protect patients' information. Mara apologized. She had spent years learning to sneak objects out of buildings. Learning where an object ought to remain was a less spectacular skill, and sometimes a harder one.","A visiting physician set up a quiet rest area. Mrs Bell had anaemia and another chronic illness; she could perform, but would decide with the doctor how much she could comfortably do that evening. Her gender, lineage, and fondness for argument were not substitutes for that assessment. The plan could not presuppose limitless energy merely because she had volunteered. Nobody would be asked to suffer from exhaustion for the sake of a touching finale.","The audience arrived carrying wet coats and a surprisingly large quantity of cucumber. The grocer had overestimated the kitchen's needs, and the surplus had become an edible donation. Agnes made sandwiches with ham for those who wanted it and an alternative for those who did not. Salt and mineral water were available without ceremony. The entire enterprise had a peculiarity that Mara found deeply attractive: people asked what other people actually needed.","A volunteer tried to call the cafe's menu an antipoverty program. Noor called it dinner. Good nutrition could support well-being without becoming a cure-all for the conditions that made people poor. A person could receive adequate food and still lack secure housing or medical care. That said, the food mattered. To acknowledge a limit was not to declare the work pointless. The distinction saved them from both extravagant praise and fashionable despair.","Mrs Bell began the show with a short scene about a guardian who lost the key to a kingdom because he refused to label his pockets. Cassian laughed first. Mara was tempted to poke him with the program. He turned his hand over on the armrest, offering the choice without looking away from the stage. She put two fingers against his palm. The room did not whirl; no music declared an inevitable destiny. Her pulse was quite inconvenient enough on its own.","The second scene concerned a cook who believed every complaint could be fixed with a larger spoon. Pip played the spoon. His costume was a cardboard vessel painted silver, and his serious bow almost brought the house down. Gideon, sitting near the front, declared that the actor understood kitchen management better than most adults. When laughter threatened to drown the next line, Mrs Bell waited. Good timing could do more than a loudspeaker.","Then a pane rattled loose in the service door. A volunteer reached for the falling frame and caught a sharp edge. The wound began to bleed. The nurse took him to the rest area, checked him, and arranged further care as needed. A bloody sleeve frightened Pip more than the man himself, so Cassian explained that a picture of blood did not tell them how serious an injury was. He did not promise that the wound was harmless before the nurse had assessed it.","The interruption complicated the show. Mrs Bell sat down while the door was made safe. She was tired, and her breath had become uneven. The physician did not allow the pressure of an audience to determine what should happen next. She chose to leave the stage for the evening. A volunteer who called this disappointing was promptly assigned to move chairs, where an insensitive remark could do less damage.","\"We shall be filled with pity,\" Mrs Bell told Mara, \"if you tell them I am bravely sacrificing my dream. I am going to have tea.\"","Mara made the announcement exactly as requested. The audience applauded the performance already given. It did not demand another. An elderly man who had once worked at a hospice offered a brief comic monologue in her place. He introduced himself as a cynic with a restricted working week. \"On Tuesdays,\" he said, \"I believe in people. This is an exceptional Tuesday.\"","His story concerned two mates who met during a bombing and spent the next fifty years arguing about the correct way to fold a blanket. One had died long before the other. The joke did not erase the grief; it made room beside it. Emotionally, Mara had expected either laughter or sadness. Their combination astonished her. Her mother's absence was not the only thing she could feel without betraying that absence.","In the middle of the monologue, an outside spotlight flashed through the window. Cassian's hand tightened. The smell of the recently heated equipment and the bright flare had caught a memory fragment. Mara noticed his change and asked whether he wanted to leave. He said he wanted a quieter place. She walked with him to the corridor, telling Ivo where they were going. She did not tell him that the moment was over before it actually was.","\"It is familiar,\" he said, staring at the open door. \"But I cannot find the rest.\"","\"You do not owe me the rest.\"","He let the sentence settle. His breathing slowed. Noor arrived, spoke with him privately, and asked Mara whether she would wait outside. She did. Affection was not a medical qualification. Cassian could ask for her company without being required to make her the only person who ever helped him.","When they returned, Pip had recruited Ivo to imitate a man on auto-pilot. The engineer walked into a painted tree, stopped, and politely asked the tree to move. The performance caused tremendous laughter. Mrs Bell said his descent into acting was a tragic loss to engineering. The spirit of the evening survived its changes because nobody had treated the original plan as more important than the people in it.","Afterward, a donor asked whether the proceeds would be sufficient to cover all repairs. Mara answered with the actual total. They could pay the inspection fee and part of the door repair. There was still money owing for the electrical work. She would not provide the audience with a false happy ending merely because it would make them feel generous. A woman at the back offered to fund the remaining door cost if the quote was independently verified.","The contractor's driver waited until most people had gone before approaching June. He had come for the show, he said, not to be a witness. A threat that there would be \"heads rolling\" had made him reluctant to question strange jobs. His invoice called several deliveries \"scene changes,\" although the loads went to the hospital entrance. He could supply copies. He did not know what was inside the cases and would not pretend otherwise.","June asked if she could record his account. He agreed, with limits on publication of his name. His cooperation did not rule out error, but it gave them something they could check. They would compare the dates with the referral records, not manufacture a perfect match by omitting inconvenient entries. Ivo found the clean tube for the banner and used a second one to protect the driver's copies from the rain.","Mara came back for her coat and found Cassian folding the last blanket. \"You did not waste the evening by needing help,\" she said. He looked up, understood what she was answering, and nodded. \"Neither did you by asking someone else to make the announcement.\" They carried the folded things to the cupboard together. Beyond the windscreen of a parked delivery van, the theatre lights were still visible. The house had failed to perform perfection. It suggests that the performance failed because it could not pay every repair bill.</button><button type=\"button\" data-option=\"1\">B. It acknowledges a useful contribution while preserving the extent of the remaining need.</button><button type=\"button\" data-option=\"2\">C. The Invoice That Stayed on the Table","volume":"3","anchor":"chapter-0032","paragraphs":["Mara's borrowed dress had pockets. This fact improved her mood more than the silk, the enviable cut, or the considerable book value of the pearl buttons. She had an appointment to appraise scenery at an auction showroom owned by the hospital's former supplier. Her invitation described her as \"the duke's artistic adviser.\" She crossed out the first two words and asked Cassian whether he objected. \"I shall try to survive the professional loss,\" he said.","Beforehand, she read the invitation's conditions. She could inspect the lots and the accompanying provenance records; she could not enter the private offices. Cassian would wait in the public gallery, where he had arranged an appointment with the compliance manager. They had requested copies of three purchasing files. Neither intended to go it alone into an unknown warehouse. Mara wanted the original invoices, not another opportunity to prove she could escape through a window.","At the front desk, a bilingual attendant gave them name badges. A charity gala rehearsal occupied one side of the showroom. A woman in a miniskirt was trying to teach a wooden astronaut to bow; the puppet's head tilted at every attempt. Its skull had been made from a broken lampshade. Behind it stood an impressive model of a cooling tower. Mara recognized the painted seam from the theatre workshop and made a note of its inventory number.","The manager, Berrit Lane, greeted them with an aggressively cheerful smile. She had worked in procurement for twenty years and expected to be way ahead of an art restorer on matters of valuation. \"We are renowned for supporting urban regeneration,\" she said. Mara asked which projects the statement was based on. Lane handed her a glossy sheet of estimated benefits. No completed project was named. Confidence, it appeared, had been asked to stand in for a date.","\"We can base our appraisal on the objects here,\" Mara said. \"Could I see their actual records?\"","Lane's expression changed by an approximate degree. It was slight but visible. \"Certainly. The documents are available for serious purchasers.\"","\"You invited an independent valuation. I understood you to consider that a serious service.\"","Cassian turned his badge around so its title was no longer visible. \"Mine is a request made through the investigation office,\" he said. \"You may answer it separately.\"","The records arrived in two boxes. Mara began with a damaged rose trellis that had belonged to the theatre. Rust marked the grained wooden base where an iron bracket had leaked. Oxide had stained the paper tag, but the date was readable. A supplier had billed for three new trellises; the same old one appeared in three promotional photographs. That discrepancy was a problem. It did not imply that every other object in the room was a fraud.","Lane said the records might be redundant. A clerk had copied old templates during a software enhancement. Mara asked whether the correction had been passed on to the purchaser. No one could find it. A mistake might originate in a careless copy, but the recipient was still entitled to an accurate bill. Lane's defense of the original error began to get in the way of the simpler task of correcting it.","Across the room, a donor objected to a poster that called all needy residents \"helpless.\" A staff member tried to explain that the phrase was psychologically effective. \"For whom?\" the donor asked. She was a retired nurse with a cheerful laugh and little tolerance for being patronized. A young employee hurried to remove the poster. Mara watched Lane's smile become a little more humble. People outside a marketing plan could be inconveniently specific.","The first patient-equipment invoice was hidden among the gala accounts. It charged for a clinic dose-monitoring unit and a video reel of a culinary demonstration. The unit had never appeared on Mercy House's inventory. A drugstore receipt attached to it supplied no corresponding purchase. Lane called this an irregular classification. Cassian asked who had authorized it. \"The old hospital board,\" she said. \"Which person?\" Her fingers stopped on the page.","She gave him a name. It matched the former administrator, but the signature beneath it did not match the stamp on the removal notice. The mismatch could prevent them from treating the two orders as a single authorization. It could also reveal a second party. Mara was far behind the certainty she had hoped to obtain, but she had stopped confusing that disappointment with defeat. A rigorous inquiry could advance by eliminating a satisfying explanation.","Lane took a phone call and left the room. The attendant leaned closer. \"They told us the hospital was closing. That these were spare assets.\" She looked distressed, then angry. \"My father was a patient there.\" Mara asked whether she would be willing to say what she had personally seen. The question was narrower than asking her to accuse her employer. After a moment, she agreed to give a statement through the proper office.","A courier entered while Cassian was recording the attendant's contact details. He carried an order to take up the boxes immediately. The office had approved their removal, he said. Mara asked for the dispatch number. He reached for her open file instead. She moved it beyond his hand. \"That is a page you have not signed for.\" He said he was in a hurry. \"Then we should not waste time making a second paperwork problem.\"","Lane returned and told them to hand the records over. The courier's authorization had been issued twenty minutes before their inspection. It did not automatically cancel the disclosure already arranged with the investigating office. Cassian called the appointed records officer. While they waited, Mara kept the boxes on the table in full view. Stealing the invoices would turn a clear dispute about access into a debate about her methods. For once, the difficult skill was to remain still.","The courier tried to nudge one box toward his cart. A cardboard doll fell from the pile of gala decorations and struck him on the shoulder. Mara caught the box before it could spill. The absurdity nearly made her laugh. His irritation did not amuse Lane. \"Would you like us to photograph the documents first?\" Mara asked. \"Then their subsequent location need not be a matter of recollection.\" Cassian could see what she was doing. He asked the officer, on the line, to confirm the permitted procedure.","Permission arrived. So did a warning that any original leaving the room required a receipt identifying its custodian. Lane had no legitimate reason to oppose photographs within the agreed inspection. A more lenient interpretation would have allowed Mara to copy additional files, but she stayed within the listed request. She wanted evidence that would survive criticism. A convenient overreach could make an accurate discovery harder to use.","One attachment contained a list of equipment deliveries organized by destination rather than date. A hospital entry had been crossed out and replaced by \"maintenance stock.\" The receiving address belonged to a harbor warehouse. No vessel was named. A column for transport costs showed a premium for temperature control. Mara photographed the whole page, including the entries that did not support her expectations. Selective clarity would be another kind of blur.","When they left, Lane asked Cassian to commend her firm's cooperation in his report. \"I shall describe what happened,\" he answered. Mara could not tell whether Lane found that favorably or unfavorably phrased. The distinction had become rather important. Outside, the attendant passed them a copy of her appointment schedule. The courier had arrived before the phone call that supposedly prompted his visit. It was a small contradiction they could now check against another record.","At Mercy House, the committee's ballot box was sitting on a kitchen stool. Residents were deciding whether the new cafe should serve supper as well as lunch. Agnes would not allow enthusiasm to substitute for staffing. Extra meals would require an additional trained worker and a reliable supply budget. A proposal that ignored those costs might pass easily and fail soon afterward. Mara put the showroom folder aside and listened to the arguments before voting.","A culinary student wanted to infuse the soup with a new spice. Gideon wanted to be certain the spice was not an experiment in disguise. A nurse checked the dietary plans before changing anything. There was no outbreak of anger, only the ordinary noisiness of people becoming accustomed to having opinions heard. Cassian asked whether his vote counted. Agnes said he was welcome to wash dishes. He regarded the distinction with a sane degree of regret.","Rowan arrived with his phone switched off. His family had threatened to withdraw support unless he removed the published purchasing accounts. He would not. The figures had been checked, and the patients' names had been redacted. His ancestry did not compel him to defend every transaction made under the family crest. It did, however, leave him with consequences he could not dismiss as somebody else's problem. He looked less like a triumphant whistleblower than a man who had lost a place to go for dinner.","Mara found an extra plate. \"We have soup,\" she said. \"It is not a financial strategy.\" \"A promising embryo of one,\" Rowan replied. His joke made her want to sympathize more carefully, rather than tell him everything would be fine. Noor asked if he wanted company or privacy. He chose the kitchen, where Pip was attempting to give the saucepan a pet name. Someone passed him bread.","Later, Cassian showed Mara a warning sent to his office. It mentioned her showroom visit. His first suggestion was an escort for every trip. She heard the old impulse and asked what specific risk the arrangement would reduce. He took a breath, looked at the message again, and separated the known facts from his fear. They agreed to travel together for the next records appointment and tell the committee where they would be. She did not agree to be watched without her knowledge.","\"I am learning that concern is a poor substitute for a question,\" he said.","\"Only if you fail to ask one.\"","\"Would you like me to stay while you finish the notes?\"","She moved her papers to make room. The invitation required no surrender of judgment. He sat beside her, close enough that their sleeves touched. At work in the kitchen, Agnes began to sing. The melody was irregular, the coffee weak, and the building still owed more money than its committee liked to admit. Mara wrote down the warehouse address.
